Along Taiwan's western coast, a small distinct subspecies of the Indo-Pacific humpback dolphin, also known as the Taiwanese white dolphin, is fighting for survival in one of the most heavily industrialized areas on the planet. A subspecies of the Indo-Pacific humpback dolphin, which can be found throughout the waters of Southeast Asia in small population clusters, are apex predators in their ecosystems, endangered only by humans.

Matsu's Fish

Stories of the dolphins helping sailors in need have seen them dubbed Matsu's fish, after the Chinese folk deity Matsu worshiped as the protector of sailors and seafarers in much of East Asia and in Taiwan in particular.
